# Dash iOS wallet user stories vs the desktop plan: gap inventory

Date: 2026-10-31. Read-only study, nothing in either repo was changed.

- **iOS source:** `~/workspace/dashwallet-ios` at `origin/develop` = `41ab11aa2` (2026-10-28). Unmerged branches were read with `git show origin/<branch>:<path>` and `git log origin/develop..origin/<branch>` (one stale remote ref, `pasta/feat/dashpay-any-balance`, is 7 commits behind its PR head `b6667aff0`, PR #1117).
- **Desktop plan:** `~/workspace/dashwallet-desktop` at `main` = `9e3add03` (2026-10-31): `docs/design/ROADMAP.md`, `docs/parity.md`, `docs/design/DASHPAY.md` (§2.9, §2.10, §5, §5a), the per-task design docs, `docs/research/03-ios-features.md` (read iOS `7c0064d6b`, 2026-10-05) and `docs/research/dashpay/ios.md` (read iOS `3df12fc89`, 2026-10-06). Desktop code under `rust/` and `apps/desktop/` was grepped where the plan says something is built.
- **Method.** Six iOS inventory passes (one per area), each reading the code and citing file:line, then six mapping passes against the plan, each reading ROADMAP/parity/DASHPAY and grepping design docs and code. Evidence for every row is in its row. The lead spot-checked these load-bearing claims in the desktop repo: contested lock constant `registration_flow.rs:78-80` (0.25 DASH), no Platform-address loop in `platform/runtime.rs:686-711`, Pay refusing shielded recipients (`screens/pay/model.ts:124-126`), `dashPayNews` only invalidating queries (`api/events.ts:73`), network switch "not yet (U-04b)" (`U-04-settings.md:37,115`, `General.tsx:284`), no X1a/X1b/X2 design docs, parity.md having no IOS-124..140 rows. Everything else is the mapping agents' reading and is as reliable as the cited file:line; items they could not verify are marked "unsure".
- **Rows.** The tables are in Parts A to F below (appendix-style, one table per sub-area). Columns: `| ID | Story | iOS evidence | iOS status | Desktop mapping | Gap |`. The Gap cell starts with a tag: COVERED (planned or built, release stated in the mapping; a COVERED row can still be unbuilt, the Gap text says so), PARTIAL (part planned), MISSING (not in plan, not excluded), EXCLUDED (plan explicitly excludes or defers with a quote), N-A (desktop differs on purpose), DEAD (iOS itself no longer has or uses it; not counted as a story).

## Counts

Counted from the Gap tags of the table rows in Parts A to F (a row is one story or a few trivially merged stories; about 10 Platform/shielded stories appear in both Part B and Part C and are counted twice):

| Part | Area | Rows | COVERED | PARTIAL | MISSING | EXCLUDED | N-A | DEAD |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | Onboarding, security, lifecycle, sync, network, settings, tools, wallets, notifications, extensions | 118 | 46 | 24 | 18 | 3 | 19 | 8 |
| B | Home, send, receive, scan/URI/BIP70, history, tax | 118 | 42 | 45 | 9 | 6 | 7 | 9 |
| C | CoinJoin, Platform addresses, Shielded, balance breakdown, send-route matrix | 117 | 36 | 46 | 20 | 0 | 10 | 5 |
| D | Identity, usernames, marketplace, credits, voting, masternodes, branch-only | 105 | 53 | 25 | 9 | 3 | 12 | 3 |
| E | DashConnect, Explore, DashSpend, Buy & Sell, DEX, CrowdNode | 83 | 23 | 22 | 3 | 24 | 4 | 7 |
| F | Contacts, profile, notifications, invitations | 89 | 54 | 16 | 3 | 2 | 2 | 12 |
| | **Total rows** | **630** | **254** | **178** | **62** | **38** | **54** | **44** |

So: **586 live stories** (630 minus 44 DEAD), of which **254 covered, 178 partial, 62 missing, 38 explicitly excluded or deferred, 54 N-A (desktop differs)**. Two cautions on reading these numbers. (1) COVERED includes stories that are planned for 1.1 or 1.2 or planned but not built (for example everything under X1b, X2, X4, MG-10, MG-13); the Gap text says which. (2) Part C used a stricter rule than the others: it counted COVERED only when a task names the story, and called it PARTIAL when the only link was an F-row citing a parity row, so its PARTIAL count is high by design, and that is the area where the owner's suspicion holds up (see "Platform and shielded: is the plan adequate?").

Summary of the iOS app on develop (for orientation): it has no PIN-encrypted seed (the mnemonic is plain Keychain bytes; the PIN only gates UI and spends), no CoinJoin mixing (only a "move mixed coins" recovery), no private-key import or sweep, no Sign Message, no invitation creation, ungated Shielded on Home/Receive, Platform addresses behind a user-visible Advanced mode, DashConnect on testnet/devnet only, CrowdNode hidden for users without an account (and a branch that deletes it). The desktop plan is ahead of iOS in places (real CoinJoin mixing, sign/verify message, invitation creation X3, ProRegTx/shared masternodes, governance, PSBT) and these are not gaps.

## Top gaps, ranked by user impact

Each line is a gap in the desktop plan with its evidence. "ROADMAP:n" means `docs/design/ROADMAP.md` line n; the rows behind each item are in the Parts.

1. **Shielded is the largest hole and is a one-line task.** iOS ships Shielded ungated on Home and Receive (`HomeBalanceView.swift:242-247`, SHLD-02) and steers new users to it; desktop 1.0 shows only a notice, X2 is a single ROADMAP row (ROADMAP:217, "3 x L", W14 to W16 after MG, ROADMAP:318-320), nothing in the engine calls shielded code (`docs/research/shielded-cost.md:91`), the vault has no Orchard or DIP-17 key scope (`dw-vault/src/signer.rs:55-90`), and the Pay screen refuses shielded recipients (`apps/desktop/src/screens/pay/model.ts:124-126`). No design doc exists for X1a, X1b or X2 (every DP, MG and U task has one). Rows: C SHLD-01..30.
2. **Platform-address support is thin even where planned.** X1a (read-only balance, the only 1.0 visibility of Platform funds) is third on the 1.0 cut list (ROADMAP:382) and no Platform-address sync loop exists (`platform/runtime.rs:686-711` returns only identity, DashPay and DPNS loops). X1b (ROADMAP:216) is one sentence: transfers, receive, advanced mode, Internal Transfer, registration from addresses. Rows: C PADDR-01..31.
3. **Platform and shielded send routes are not enumerated.** iOS executes eight routes plus identity routes (SEND-08, ROUTE-01..23). The plan names none individually: shielded to external shielded, shielded to Platform, Platform to shielded, Platform to Core withdraw (full and partial forms), Core to own Platform, external Platform send. Max drains are also unplanned: Core to Shielded Max (#1205, 2026-10-21) and Max to an external shielded address (#1251, 2026-10-28). Rows: B SEND-08/14/16, C ROUTE-*, SHLD-05/06.
4. **No history for Platform or shielded activity.** iOS lists incoming Platform payments and all shielded operations in history with memo, route pills and "Shielded sent/received" filters (HOME-44, HOME-45, SHLD-13..16, PADDR-07). Parity IOS-035 and IOS-028's shielded part have no ROADMAP owner. A Platform balance would show in 1.0 with no activity behind it. Rows: B HOME-44/45, TX-11; C SHLD-13..16.
5. **Stuck-transfer recovery for funding locks of types 4 and 5 is unowned.** iOS has "Pending, tap to finish", Finish now, and a bulk Finish after restore (SHLD-17..19, PADDR-24..26, TX-09, SYNC-09). Desktop's built "Finish transfers" handles registration and top-up only (`registration.rs:309-319`); `E0-04-grants-leases.md:2968` says "the X-phase task adds `register`" for shielded redrives but no task does.
6. **Identity funding and top-up from Platform or Shielded are in no X row.** iOS tops up from Core, Platform or Shielded (two-step for Shielded), withdraws credits to own Core or Platform address, and funds registration from Platform (Advanced) or Shielded with a readiness checklist (3-hour note rest, 250-note pool) (CRED-03/04/05/08, PADDR-19/20/21, SHLD-26, ID-05..09). The plan's DP1-06/DP1-10 top-up is Core-funded; X1b and X2 name registration funding only. Rows: D CRED-*, ID-05..09; C PADDR-19..21.
7. **Contested-name cost is stale in the desktop engine.** iOS #1199 (2026-10-21) follows the protocol version (0.15 DASH from PV14, 0.25 before); desktop hard-codes the 0.25 DASH lock (`rust/crates/dw-engine/src/platform/registration_flow.rs:78-80`, DP1-09 D4) and the invitation contested tier (`invitation_check.rs:90`), so it over-locks about 0.10 DASH on PV14+ and mis-tiers invitations. Rows: D NAME-20, ID-07, INV-11 (F).
8. **CoinJoin to Shielded has no consolidation, input cap or finality handling.** iOS #1221 (2026-10-27) consolidates above 50 UTXOs because the asset-lock cap is 100 inputs and Platform's 20,480 B limit binds from about 65 (platform#4460). Desktop does real mixing and so produces many UTXOs; X2 says only "move mixed coins to Shielded" and the engine's Shielded destination returns `NotImplemented` (`coinjoin.rs:2234,2274`). Rows: C CJ-15..18.
9. **Pay-to-contact is Core/CoinJoin only on desktop, and the iOS roadmap is moving past that.** iOS branches add any-balance payment from Platform or Shielded (PR #1117, branch-only), exact-fee review (PR #1214) and fiat entry (`fix/contact-pay-fiat`); the desktop plan (DASHPAY §2.3, DP3-01) covers none, and fiat depends on MP-01 which is cut candidate 7 (ROADMAP:386). Rows: F CON-32..40.
10. **The identity-verify link exclusion is no longer justified.** DASHPAY:1108 and :1131 exclude it "(no contract)"; iOS #1141 (2026-10-08) publishes and reads an `identityVerify` document on mainnet and testnet (`IdentityVerifyService.swift:79-80,178`). Rows: D NAME-08, NAME-16, VOTE-06.
11. **No protocol-compatibility advisory.** iOS #1232 (2026-10-27) shows a "network uses a newer protocol" banner; desktop has nothing and an unknown protocol version makes `cost_table` return `internal` (D agent). Rows: A LIFE-10, B HOME-15, D NAME-22/23.
12. **Wallet-open failure handling is a generic splash.** iOS #1195 and #1200 (2026-10-08 to 10-12) classify failures (keys card, disk full, corruption), offer backup from the failure card and a "reset local data and rescan". Desktop shows a generic retry. Rows: A LIFE-02..07.
13. **No send guard while a restored wallet is still syncing.** iOS #998 plus #1238 (2026-10-23, armed on keychain recovery too); nothing in `dw-engine/src/send` or the Pay screen. Rows: A ONB-22, B SEND-17.
14. **OS notifications are not delivered and have no toggle.** The engine emits `DashPayNews` but the renderer only invalidates queries (`api/events.ts:73`); DP2-09 F4 handed delivery to retired E0-11 (ROADMAP:81); W-02 covers Windows toasts only, so macOS and Linux have no owner; Settings shows a "Not yet" row. Rows: A NOTIF-01/03, SET-05; F NOTIF-07, NOTIF-10.
15. **Settings and support gaps in the Tauri app.** No in-app network switch (U-04b is in no ROADMAP or backlog row, `U-04-settings.md:37,115`); no log export or support-email path (`About.tsx:40` points at U-05 which has no such row); backup reminder and "needs backup" state deferred circularly between U-02 D13 and U-03 D16. Rows: A NET-01, SET-08, SET-10, ONB-13/14.
16. **DashConnect v2 is not recorded in the plan.** F24 (DASHPAY:1127) says "revisit with Connect v2"; v2 is concrete on 12 stacked iOS branches (one-QR, per-key budgets and expiry, on-chain revoke, `dashpay://connect` carrier, DIP #181), still testnet-only. The Tauri app registers the `dashpay` scheme but has no arm for `connect`/`sign` links (`deeplink.rs:125-153`). `docs/research/dashpay/platform.md:229` wrongly calls DashConnect "not a shipped iOS feature". Rows: E DC-*.
17. **Username marketplace X4 and extra-name flows are one line.** Buying a listed name from the registration form (under 10 DASH), registering a second or contested name on an identity that has one, Browse, and the instant-username offer at contest confirmation have no owner; desktop's instant name is in My Profile only. Rows: D NAME-06/07/10, MKT-*.
18. **Invitation claim entry points.** No persistent Home card for a pending invitation and no invitation-QR scan (Add Contact's scanner rejects non-contact codes); desktop shows "Links you opened" once per launch. iOS invitation creation does not exist (desktop X3 is ahead). Rows: F INV-04, INV-06.
19. **Balance hero and breakdown are undefined.** The plan never says whether Platform or Shielded count in the hero total; the Overview strip (Available/Pending/Immature) is a different split from iOS's per-pool card; "advanced mode" in X1b contradicts MG-00 D2 ("Show Masternodes" instead of a general advanced mode, `MG-00-masternodes-ux.md:100`). Shortcut-bar customization has no task. Rows: B HOME-01..07, 17..22; C BAL-*.
20. **CrowdNode has no recorded decision.** MP-13 says "withdraw-only, 1.2, or drop"; parity IOS-091 still lists the full scope; iOS `feat/remove-crowdnode` (2026-10-30) deletes everything (-29.8k lines), while develop still serves existing accounts. An iOS-seeded wallet with a CrowdNode balance has no desktop exit unless MP-13 ships. Rows: E CN-*.
21. **Lock-screen and biometric stories have no owner.** Quick Receive and Scan to Send on the lock screen, biometric UI and the 7-day / spend-limit rules (engine has them) wait on external B4 or nothing. Rows: A SEC-05..10.
22. **Smaller unowned items:** testnet faucet in the Tauri app (E), evonode "Nodes" shortcut and epoch-block activity (D MN-09/10), "Internal Transfer" tax category in MP-04 (E MISC-03), swap rows in history (#1196, E SWAP-10), clipboard address suggestion and QR-image decode in Pay (B SEND-05, SCAN-03), shared-profile avatar privacy (contacts' `http://` avatars show initials because desktop is HTTPS-only, F PROF-07).

Not gaps (desktop differs on purpose, or desktop is ahead): PIN vs passphrase vault; CoinJoin mixing (iOS deleted it, desktop U-06 ships it); sign/verify message, PSBT, coin control; invitation creation (X3, iOS has none); ProRegTx/shared masternodes/governance (iOS has no ProRegTx); operator-signed Unban stays excluded (CLAUDE.md "Leave to the Rust node").

## Platform and shielded: is the plan adequate?

The owner's suspicion holds. From Part C (full text there, with ROADMAP line numbers):

- **Size.** X1a is "M + S" (about 3 to 4 agent-days, DASHPAY:609), X1b "2 x L" (8 to 10 days, ROADMAP:216, :360), X2 "3 x L" (12 to 15 days, ROADMAP:217, :360): roughly 23 to 29 agent-days against 48 to 68 for MG and 76 to 109 for DP1 to DP6 (ROADMAP:355, :360). A single resumable money flow cost an L engine task plus an L UI task in DashPay (DP1-02 + DP1-09; DP3-01 + DP3-06). X1b + X2 must deliver about 12 iOS routes, 4 recovery flows, a receive rail per balance, Internal Transfer, advanced mode, two registration funding sources and the readiness checklist; by the DashPay analogy that is nearer 35 to 50 agent-days (the Part C agent's estimate, not a plan figure).
- **Hidden prerequisites in no task:** vault signer scopes for DIP-17 and Orchard keys (E0-03 at ROADMAP:65 names only identity, DashPay and funding scopes); E0-04 Q15 registration of shielded redrives; credit-denominated `PlatformOp` caps for address transfers; recovery for lock types 4 and 5; a cached viewing key so shielded sync runs while the vault is locked; T2/T3 suites and a kill matrix for X1b (only "X2 testnet shield/unshield suite", ROADMAP:319).
- **Scheduling.** X2 lands last (W14 to W16) after MG; X1a is a cut candidate; 1.0 will show Platform funds read-only with no history, and shielded funds only as a notice (DASHPAY:610), while iOS users hold shielded funds by default.
- **Measured cost of halo2** (E0-12, `shielded-cost.md:14-17`): +6 percent build CPU, +0.23 to 0.40 MB release binary, 3.2 to 3.5 s prover warm-up; so the "decide later" argument for deferring a read-only shielded balance is weaker than DASHPAY:610 assumed.
- **Recommendation (from Part C):** split X1b and X2 into per-route engine and UI tasks each with a design doc; add explicit tasks for history and receipts, recovery, sync info, vault key scopes, Max drains and CoinJoin consolidation; make X1a non-cuttable; reconsider a read-only shielded balance in 1.0.

| Date | PR / commit | Change | Effect on the desktop plan |
|---|---|---|---|
| 10-08 | #1141 (commits 08-24 to 10-07) | Join sheet rebuilt: funding-source privacy page (Shielded / Dash / Platform), explicit source confirmation, instant (temporary) username offer, contested confirmation, **identity-verify link**, Home/More registration status rows (Creating, Voting, Approved, Rejected, Blocked), vote change and 5-cast ceiling, Governance moved behind Advanced | funding picker, verify-link exclusion (DASHPAY:1131) |
| 10-08 | #1162 | Voting-key import UI | MG-10 "Add key" |
| 10-08 to 10-12 | #1195, #1200, 6ca4b8d7e | Wallet-open failure cards (keys card, disk full), backup from failure card, reset local data and rescan with durable per-scope intents; CoinJoin rescan durability (52a94122b, 79417a150, 10-09) | LIFE-02..07 |
| 10-10 | #1150, #1177 | Username pick survives relaunch; failed-transfer toast persists | minor |
| 10-20 | #1156 | Invitation claim reworked: Keychain-backed Home card, paste field removed, tiers by amount | INV-05/06/11 |
| 10-20 | #1173 | Mainnet OneLink (`dashpay.onelink.me`) universal link | DP5-01 (desktop handles OneLink generically, `deeplink.rs:469-485`) |
| 10-20 | #1196, #1203, #1215 | Buy swap order row in history once deposit is on chain; imported wallet scans full history (chain-store rebuild); `dashwallet://pay=` scan | swap history, restore |
| 10-21 | #1199 | Contested-name cost follows protocol version (0.15 DASH from PV14) | `registration_flow.rs:80` |
| 10-21 | #1205 | Core to Shielded Max made restart-safe | X2 Max |
| 10-21 to 10-27 | #1222, #1232 | Testnet pinned to PV14, then auto-detect; advisory banner replaces blocking checks | no desktop counterpart |
| 10-22 | 48d1014e9, 26b932d56 | Unconfirmed identity transfer outcomes announced | recovery |
| 10-23 | #1238 | Keychain recovery arms the initial-restore send gate | restore guard |
| 10-27 | #1221 | CoinJoin consolidate-before-shield, finality gates | X2 |
| 10-28 | #1251 | Max to an external shielded address via SDK wallet drain | X2 Max |

Other staleness found: `docs/research/dashpay/platform.md:229` says DashConnect is "not a shipped iOS feature" (it is, testnet/devnet only, since 2026-07-30); `ios.md:252` cites dips#191 for App Connect (it is #181); research 03:143 "Platform routes ADV" is wrong on develop (Send-tab Platform routes are not Advanced-gated, `SendViewModel.swift:662`); the "CrowdNode suspended" wording understates what iOS still serves; the iOS `README.md` feature list and `DEVELOPER-NOTES.md` crash-reporting section are themselves stale. Desktop-side hygiene: `docs/parity.md` has no rows IOS-124..140 although DASHPAY F6/F15 cite them; IOS-057, QT-041..050, IOS-077 and the MG rows (IOS-080..082, QT-118..134) lag the merged code (U-06, DP5-02/03, MG engine, MG-09, MG-11); parity rows IOS-005/013/051/105/106/112/117 describe the frozen Swift apps and overstate the Tauri app.

## Open questions and uncertainty

- Counts are tag counts over agent-written rows; the tag boundaries (COVERED versus PARTIAL for "implied only") are judgments, stated in each Part. The lead re-verified the ten desktop-code claims listed under Method; the rest rely on the cited file:line.
- iOS items the inventories flagged "unsure": non-English BIP-39 restore at the pinned rust-dashcore rev (ONB-17), which target (dashwallet or dashpay) ships to the App Store and so whether the Watch app is live (EXT-01), whether the Platform "advanced" gate also hides Send-tab routes (inventory says no), the exact exclusion regex for contested names (lives in Rust), and ONB-21 on desktop (Add Wallet rescan, unverified).
- Branch maturity for the DashConnect stack is moving daily (tips on 11-01).

# Part A: onboarding, security, lifecycle, sync, network, settings, tools, wallets, notifications, extensions

# Area A: onboarding, security, lifecycle, sync, network, settings, tools, multi-wallet, notifications, extensions, docs

Inventory: dashwallet-ios origin/develop 41ab11aa2 (2026-10-28). Desktop: dashwallet-desktop main 9e3add03 (2026-10-31).
Legend for paths: `S/` = `DashWallet/Sources/`; `SDK/` = `S/Infrastructure/SwiftDashSDK/`. iOS evidence is the leading refs of each input row, verbatim (trailing refs trimmed; see the input inventory for the full list).
"Tauri" = the desktop app in `apps/desktop` + `rust/crates/dw-app`. `docs/parity.md` IOS rows were measured on the frozen Swift apps (MacUI/CrossUI), not on Tauri (U-01 D17, U-04 D20), so a parity "done" can be unbuilt in Tauri; I checked the Tauri code where it mattered.
Two inventory statuses were wrong and are corrected below after spot checks on origin/develop: NOTIF-06 (background refresh is paused in the build: `BackgroundRefreshCoordinator.swift:192 isEnabledInThisBuild = false`) and EXT-02 (the Today extension is in no embed phase of `DashWallet.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj`; only `WatchApp.app` and `WatchApp Extension.appex` are embedded).
Tag convention: COVERED = a task or built code covers the story (the release is named when it is not 1.0); a planned-for-1.1/1.2 row is COVERED with the release stated.

## Area summary

Counts over 118 rows (126 input IDs; 8 merges: ONB-16+17, ONB-19+20, SEC-06+07+08, SEC-22+23, LIFE-02+04, NET-04+05, SET-10+TOOL-04 counted as one each; TOOL-04 lives in SET-10):

COVERED 46 | PARTIAL 24 | MISSING 18 | EXCLUDED 3 | N-A 19 | DEAD 8

Per sub-area (COVERED/PARTIAL/MISSING/EXCLUDED/N-A/DEAD): ONB 9/3/4/0/4/0; SEC 10/3/2/0/6/0; LIFE 1/3/3/0/2/0; SYNC 5/5/4/0/0/0; NET 2/0/1/0/3/0; SET 6/4/2/0/3/1; TOOL 4/1/0/1/0/2; MW 6/0/2/0/0/0; NOTIF 1/3/0/2/0/1; EXT 1/2/0/0/0/2; DOC 1/0/0/0/1/2.

Note that many COVERED rows are covered by a task that is not yet built or is later than 1.0: MP-01 (SET-03, SET-04), MP-03 (SET-11, cuttable), MP-14 (SET-12, cuttable to English only), MP-15 (TOOL-05, cuttable), MP-06 (ONB-19+20, 1.2), MG-10 (TOOL-02, 1.1), MG (SET-14, 1.1), MP-11/12 (disabled until B1).

## Top gaps in this area

1. No in-app network switch (NET-01): General says to relaunch with `--network`; "U-04b" is a follow-up in no ROADMAP or backlog row (U-04-settings.md:164).
2. No OS notifications in Tauri (NOTIF-01, NOTIF-03, SET-05): the notifier is bound only in the frozen dw-ffi, DP2-09's F4 waits on the retired E0-11, macOS has no notifier at all.
3. No backup-reminder / needs-backup state (ONB-13, ONB-14, MW-07): U-03 D16 and U-02 D13 each defer to the other, and the "Back up" shortcut is permanently disabled (`Overview.tsx:106`).
4. No support or log-export path (SET-08, SET-10+TOOL-04, LIFE-07): About points at U-05 (`About.tsx:40`), which has no such row; the engine's `logs::export` is unbound in dw-app.
5. No send guard while a restored wallet is still syncing (ONB-22): none in `dw-engine/src/send` or Pay; iOS added it in #998 and #1238.
6. Platform-address and shielded sync/account visibility is unplanned (SYNC-09, SYNC-10, SYNC-12, MW-06): X1a is a balance line only, X2 omits stuck-transfer recovery and sync info, and there is no per-wallet accounts view.
7. Failed-open recovery is a generic splash (LIFE-02+04, LIFE-05, LIFE-06): iOS #1195/#1200 added classified cards with backup and reset-and-rescan.
8. Biometric unlock has no UI path or date (ONB-06, SEC-05, SEC-06+07+08, SEC-10): the quick-unlock engine, 7-day rule and limits exist, but macOS waits on external B4, and Quick Receive / Scan to Send on the lock screen are unowned (U-02 D10).
9. No protocol-advisory banner (LIFE-10): new in iOS #1232 (2026-10-27); PK-03 updates are a different mechanism.
10. No lock-screen wipe (SEC-18): a user without passphrase or phrase can only delete the data root by hand.
11. Phrase repair is 1.2 (ONB-19+20, MP-06) and the iOS intro carousel is unplanned (ONB-01).
12. The tray menu has no balance, QR, request or last-transaction content (EXT-01), so the Watch/Today replacement story is only a menu of navigation shortcuts.

## Stale research

- `docs/research/03-ios-features.md` (read at 7c0064d6b, 2026-10-05) vs develop 41ab11aa2:
  - Wallet-open failure UX is newer than the research: keys card (LIFE-03, #1200, 2026-10-12), backup from a failure card (LIFE-05, #1195, 2026-10-08), "Reset wallet data and rescan" with durable per-scope intents (LIFE-06, #1195 and 6ca4b8d7e, 2026-10-08). Research §1.1 line 81 lists only "failed open".
  - Protocol-compatibility advisory banner (LIFE-10, ba3a09547 2026-10-21, final #1232 2026-10-27) is absent from the research.
  - Import now rebuilds the SPV chain store so a full history is scanned (ONB-21, #1203, 2026-10-20) and keychain recovery arms the first-sync send gate per network (ONB-22, #1238, 2026-10-23).
  - Home-card accept for invitation links (EXT-04, #1156, 2026-10-20) is newer than research §1.1 line 83.
  - More-menu icons changed (SET-01, #1161, 2026-10-08); no behaviour change.
  - Research 03:317 ("BackgroundRefreshCoordinator is disabled") is still right: inventory NOTIF-06 saying shipped is wrong (`isEnabledInThisBuild = false`).
  - Research 03:322 ("Today extension not embedded in either app target") is still right; inventory EXT-02 "partly wired / embedded by dashwallet" is wrong (only the Watch is embedded).
  - Research 03:80 and 03:81 treat the migration as iOS-only legacy; develop still ships it (ONB-02, LIFE-08), still N-A for desktop.
- `docs/research/dashpay/ios.md`: not materially affected by this area.
- iOS-side docs inside the inventory that are themselves stale (DEAD): `README.md` feature list (DOC-03) and `DEVELOPER-NOTES.md` crash-reporting section (DOC-04).

- `About.tsx:40` and `U-04-settings.md:91` say Support, source code and log export "come with Tools (U-05)"; U-05-tools.md has no such row.
- U-03 D16 defers the "Back up" shortcut to U-02, and U-02 D13 defers recording "backed up" to U-03 Home (ONB-13).
- DP2-09 F4 (`DP2-09-contact-pane.md:96`) hands OS notifications to "E0-11 notifier"; ROADMAP:81 retires E0-11.
- `docs/parity.md` IOS rows (005, 013, 051, 105, 106, 112, 117) describe the frozen Swift apps; for Tauri they overstate (IOS-051 refuses while syncing; IOS-106 network switch; IOS-112 log export) (IOS-001, the carousel, is the only one that reads partial for a reason that still holds).

# Part B: home, send, receive, scan/URI/BIP70, history, tax

# Mapping B: Home, Send, Receive, Scan/URI/BIP70, Transactions, Tax

Source inventory: `ios-inventory-raw/B_home.md` (dashwallet-ios origin/develop 41ab11aa2, 2026-10-28). Desktop repo: dashwallet-desktop main 9e3add03 (2026-10-31).
`S/` = `DashWallet/Sources/` in the iOS evidence column. Doc cites are `file:line` under `docs/design/` unless stated (`ROADMAP.md`, `DASHPAY.md`, `U-01-shell.md`, `U-03-wallet-screens.md`, `UX-SPEC.md`); `parity.md` is `docs/parity.md`.

How the Platform and shielded rows are covered (the lead asked for precision):
- **X1a (1.0)**, ROADMAP.md:215: "Read-only Platform-address balance (`platform_address_sync`), a breakdown line with info sheet (MP-02), and the restore notice for shielded balances". Nothing else.
- **X1b (1.1)**, ROADMAP.md:216: "Platform-address transfers, receive, advanced mode, Internal Transfer with a non-Core confirm, registration funded from addresses". DASHPAY.md:611 adds that X1b shares the advanced-mode and Internal Transfer screens with X2.
- **X2 (1.1)**, ROADMAP.md:217: "Shielded: feature on, balance, shield/unshield, shielded registration funding with the readiness checklist, move mixed coins to Shielded". It does NOT name: a shielded receive address, shielded-to-shielded or shielded-to-Platform sends, shielded history rows, or stuck-transfer recovery.
- X1a, X1b and X2 have no per-task design doc (only the ROADMAP row and DASHPAY.md §2.10 / F25 / F26 at :1128-1129).
- Built today: `dw-uri` classifies Core / Platform (DIP-18 bech32m) / shielded addresses (`apps/desktop/src/api/generated/AddressClass.ts`, `rust/crates/dw-uri/src/keyio.rs:231-277`). Pay REJECTS Platform and shielded destinations with a message (`apps/desktop/src/screens/pay/model.ts:123-126,147`). `dw-engine` has a non-default `shielded` feature that nothing calls yet (`rust/crates/dw-engine/Cargo.toml:11-14`, `docs/research/shielded-cost.md:91`).
- Another agent maps these from the C inventory; rows here are mapped in full anyway.

## Area summary

Counts (rows as merged in the tables above; 118 table rows covering all 125 inventory IDs):

COVERED: 42 / PARTIAL: 45 / MISSING: 9 / EXCLUDED: 6 / N-A: 7 / DEAD: 9

Platform and shielded rows specifically (HOME-01/02/05/06/07/39/43/44/45/55/56, SEND-04/07/08/14/16/20, RECV-01/04):
- 1.0 delivers only a read-only Platform balance, a breakdown line and a shielded restore notice (X1a).
- 1.1 delivers Platform transfers, Platform receive, an advanced mode, Internal Transfer (X1b), plus shielded balance, shield/unshield, registration funding and move-mixed-coins (X2).
- No plan row names: Platform or shielded history rows; shielded receive address; shielded to shielded, shielded to Platform or Platform to shielded sends; Max on non-Core routes; stuck-shielded-transfer recovery; shielded filter chips; the route pills; a per-route progress checklist.

## Top gaps in this area

1. Platform and shielded activity rows (HOME-44, HOME-45, TX-11): no row in X1a/X1b/X2 (ROADMAP.md:215-217), only parity IOS-035 "not started M4". A Platform balance shows in 1.0 with no history behind it.
2. Shielded receive and the receive-side watcher (RECV-01, RECV-04): X2 lists "feature on, balance, shield/unshield" (ROADMAP.md:217), not a receive address. X1b has "receive" for Platform only. Live watching is deferred (UX-SPEC.md:616).
3. Advanced-mode design contradiction (HOME-06): X1b lists "advanced mode" (:216) but MG-00 D2 says none exists (MG-00-masternodes-ux.md:100). X1a shows Platform in 1.0 with no gate, then X1b adds one in 1.1.
4. Hero total and breakdown semantics (HOME-01, HOME-02, HOME-05): the plan never says whether Platform/Shielded enter the hero total. The current breakdown strip (Available/Pending/Immature) is a different split from iOS's per-pool card.
5. Send routes not named in the plan (SEND-08, SEND-14, SEND-16): shieldedToShielded, shieldedToPlatform, platformToShielded, platformToCore; Send-form step checklist; non-Core Max (#1251 newer).
6. Shortcut bar customization (HOME-17..22): Tauri has four fixed slots (`Overview.tsx:102-107`); the Swift implementation is frozen and retired at U-08; no ROADMAP task.
7. Protocol-compatibility advisory banner (HOME-15): not in plan; landed 10-21/10-23; relevant to the Platform protocol-version bumps.
8. Pay form intake (SEND-05, SCAN-03, HOME-24/25): no clipboard suggestion, no QR image decode and no lock-screen scan or Quick Receive (RECV-06, U-02 D10) in Pay; only Add Contact decodes images.
9. Transaction-detail tooling in Tauri (TX-05, TX-06, TX-07, TX-12): raw tx exists in the binding (`TxDetails.ts:14`) but is not rendered; explorer links are explicitly deferred (U-03 D15) with no owning task.
10. Moving mixed coins (HOME-51): Shielded destination in 1.1 (X2) but wallet sweep in 1.2 (MP-08); engine shielded branch is `not_implemented`; #1221 consolidate-before-shield not mirrored.
11. Sync failure and phase UI (HOME-13, HOME-14): status bar only; no per-phase dialog, Retry strip or Change peers in Tauri.
12. Standing invitation card on Home (HOME-54): only a one-shot claim at launch/unlock (DP5-03:33-34); iOS #1156 added a persistent card.

- #1251 (10-28): Max to an external Shielded address uses the SDK wallet drain (SEND-16). Research §1.5 has fee-aware Max only generically.
- #1221 (10-27): CoinJoin consolidate-before-shield (HOME-51). Research §1.8 (03:177) says "Threshold > 1000 duffs"; develop uses max(100_000, recoveryDust).

- #1205 (10-21): Core to Shielded Max made restart-safe (SEND-16).

# Part C: CoinJoin, Platform addresses, Shielded, balance breakdown, send-route matrix

# C — CoinJoin, Platform addresses, Shielded, Home balance, send routes: iOS → desktop plan map

Inputs: iOS inventory `ios-inventory-raw/C_cj.md` (dashwallet-ios origin/develop 41ab11aa2, 2026-10-28). Desktop: `dashwallet-desktop` main 9e3add03 (2026-10-31), read-only.
Shorthands: `ROADMAP` = docs/design/ROADMAP.md, `DASHPAY` = docs/design/DASHPAY.md, `P` = docs/parity.md, `S/` = iOS `DashWallet/Sources/`.

**What the plan actually says for this area** (there are NO design docs for X1a, X1b or X2; only these one-liners, DASHPAY §2.10 and F3/F25/F26):
- **X1a** (1.0, M + S, ROADMAP:215): "Read-only Platform-address balance (`platform_address_sync`), a breakdown line with info sheet (MP-02), and the restore notice for shielded balances".
- **X1b** (1.1, 2 × L, ROADMAP:216): "Platform-address transfers, receive, advanced mode, Internal Transfer with a non-Core confirm, registration funded from addresses".
- **X2** (1.1, 3 × L, ROADMAP:217): "Shielded: feature on, balance, shield/unshield, shielded registration funding with the readiness checklist, "move mixed coins to Shielded"".
- F25 (DASHPAY:1128) ties X1a/X1b to parity IOS-045, 047, 053, 059…064; F26 (DASHPAY:1129) ties X2 to IOS-059…062 and says "never the default registration funding; 1.0 says shielded balances are not shown yet".
- Tagging rule used here: a story is COVERED only if an X/DP/MP/U task's own words name it (or it is built). If it is only reachable through an F-row citing a parity row (e.g. F26 → IOS-060 → "shielded → shielded"), it is PARTIAL ("implied only").

**Built today (grepped rust/ and apps/):** E0-12's non-default `shielded` cargo feature + `examples/shielded_warmup.rs` (nothing in dw-engine calls shielded code: shielded-cost.md:91); `classify_address` → Core/Platform/Shielded (m1-engine.md:505, dw-uri `keyio.rs:82-87` DIP-18 0xb0/0x80, shielded 0x10); the Pay screen refuses Platform and shielded recipients ("This wallet cannot pay shielded addresses yet", apps/desktop/src/screens/pay/model.ts:124-126); a `SyncLoop::PlatformAddressSync` enum value (platform/startup.rs:99) and a DP6-03 label for it, but `platform_loops()` returns only identity/DashPay/DPNS (platform/runtime.rs:686-711), so **no Platform-address sync runs**; CoinJoin recovery scan and "move mixed coins" to the wallet in the engine and dw-app (coinjoin.rs:2145-2300, dw-app api/coinjoin.rs:671-696), with `Shielded` returning `NotImplemented` (coinjoin.rs:2234, :2274) and **no renderer caller**; real CoinJoin mixing UI (U-06, merge 3198a0a7). The vault has no signer scope for DIP-17 Platform-address keys or Orchard keys (dw-vault/src/signer.rs:55-90 lists PlatformIdentity, DashPayCrypto, PlatformFunding, MasternodeVoting, MasternodeOwner).

## CJ — CoinJoin

Desktop differs fundamentally: it ships **real CoinJoin mixing** (crate `dw-coinjoin`, engine m3-engine.md §2.1, UI task U-06 at ROADMAP:130, merged), while iOS deleted mixing and keeps only a recovery sweep. Parity IOS-058 is decided **IN** (m3-engine.md:87).

| ID | Story (as a user) | iOS evidence | iOS status | Desktop mapping | Gap |

## Platform/shielded plan adequacy

- **Size.** X1a is "M + S" (ROADMAP:215), about 3–4 agent-days (DASHPAY:609), budgeted with MP-01/03/14/15 at 8–12 days (ROADMAP:356). X1b is "2 × L" = 8–10 days (ROADMAP:216, :360). X2 is "3 × L" = 12–15 days (ROADMAP:217, :360). That is about 23–29 agent-days for everything in this area, against 48–68 for MG and 76–109 for DP1–DP6 (ROADMAP:355, :360).
- **Comparison with similar DP work.** A single resumable money flow cost an L engine task plus an L UI task: DP1-02 + DP1-09 (ROADMAP:150, :157), and DP3-01 + DP3-06 likewise. X1b + X2 must deliver about 12 iOS routes, 4 asset-lock or proof recovery flows, a receive rail per balance, an Internal Transfer screen, advanced mode, two new registration funding sources and the readiness checklist. By the DP analogy that is about 10–14 tasks, roughly 35–50 agent-days. 20–25 days is roughly half of that (my estimate, not a plan figure).
- **Scope.** Of the 96 non-CoinJoin rows here (99 inventory stories), the X rows name about 20. In this file, PADDR + SHLD + BAL + ROUTE come to 31 COVERED, 41 PARTIAL and 15 MISSING. PARTIAL is mostly "implied only": an F25/F26 parity citation that no task owns.
- **Wholly unowned (MISSING):** Platform and shielded history rows, memo and filters (IOS-028, IOS-035); receipts and notifications; shielded and Platform-address sync info (IOS-114); restored-history honesty; Core Max drain and external shielded Max (#1205, #1251); CoinJoin consolidation and the 100-input cap (#1221).
- **Hidden prerequisites in no task:**
  - vault signer scopes for DIP-17 address keys and for Orchard spend and viewing keys (signer.rs:55-90; E0-03 at ROADMAP:65 names only identity, DashPay and funding scopes);
  - E0-04 Q15's "the X-phase task adds `register`" for shielded redrives (E0-04-grants-leases.md:2968);
  - credit-denominated `PlatformOp` caps for address transfers;
  - recovery for funding lock types 4 and 5 (desktop "Finish transfers" handles registration and top-up only, registration.rs:309-319);
  - a cached viewing key so shielded sync runs while the vault is locked (like DP2-10's xpub);
  - test suites: only "X2 testnet shield/unshield suite" (ROADMAP:319), with no T2/T3 suite or kill matrix for X1b.
- **No design docs.** There is none for X1a, X1b or X2, while every DP, MG and U task has one. The UX spec also contradicts X1b: MG-00 D2 chose "Show Masternodes" "instead of a general 'advanced mode'" (MG-00-masternodes-ux.md:100; UX-SPEC.md:878).
- **Product fit vs iOS.** iOS ships Shielded ungated, on Home by default, on the receive screen of every user, and steers new users to shielded funding (DASHPAY:610 says so). Desktop 1.0 shows no shielded balance, only a notice. X2 lands last, in W14–W16, after MG (ROADMAP:318-320), and F26 rules shielded funding never the default (DASHPAY:1129). X1a, the only 1.0 visibility of Platform funds, is third on the 1.0 cut list (ROADMAP:382) and not started (runtime.rs:686-711).
- **Desktop-specific risk.** Desktop mixes for real (U-06), so its users will hold many denominated UTXOs. X2's "move mixed coins to Shielded" will then hit the 100-input asset-lock cap and the 20,480 B transition limit, unless the consolidation work from iOS #1221 is added.
- **Verdict:** the X rows are not sufficient in size or scope.
  - Split X1b and X2 into per-route engine and UI tasks, each with a design doc.
  - Add explicit tasks for history and receipts, recovery, sync info, vault key scopes, Max drains and consolidation.
  - Make X1a non-cuttable.
  - Reconsider whether 1.0 ships a read-only shielded balance, given the halo2 cost E0-12 measured: +6 % build CPU, +0.23–0.40 MB release binary, 3.5 s warm-up (shielded-cost.md:14-17).

## Area summary

Counts over 117 rows (CJ 21, PADDR 31, SHLD 27, BAL 11, ROUTE 27):

| Tag | CJ | PADDR | SHLD | BAL | ROUTE | Total |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| COVERED | 5 | 12 | 5 | 5 | 9 | **36** |
| PARTIAL | 5 | 13 | 13 | 3 | 12 | **46** |
| MISSING | 5 | 4 | 9 | 0 | 2 | **20** |
| EXCLUDED | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | 0 | **0** |
| N-A | 4 | 1 | 0 | 2 | 3 | **10** |
| DEAD | 2 | 1 | 0 | 1 | 1 | **5** |

## Top gaps in this area

1. **No shielded support in 1.0.** iOS ships Shielded ungated, on Home and Receive by default, and steers new users into it. Desktop shows only a notice until X2, which lands in W14–W16 after MG (DASHPAY:610, :1129; ROADMAP:217, :318-320). Same-seed users will see a missing balance.
2. **Platform funds are read-only until 1.1, and even that is fragile.** X1a is the third cut candidate (ROADMAP:382) and not built: there is no Platform-address loop (runtime.rs:686-711).
3. **No history for Platform and shielded activity.** Activity rows, memo, Shielded filters, restored-history honesty, receipts and incoming notifications (IOS-028/035/054/116) are owned by no task, so X1b and X2 would move money with no history rows.
4. **Stuck-transfer recovery for X1b and X2 funding locks (types 4 and 5)** — "Pending — tap to finish", Finish now, bulk Finish after restore — is only implied (F26 → IOS-061). Desktop's built "Finish transfers" covers registration and top-up only (registration.rs:309-319).
5. **Key and engine prerequisites are unlisted:** vault scopes for DIP-17 and Orchard keys (signer.rs:55-90), E0-04 Q15 registration of shielded redrives (E0-04:2968), locked-vault viewing keys, and X1b and X2 test suites.
6. **CoinJoin → Shielded has no consolidation, 100-input cap or finality gates** (#1221). This is worse on desktop, where real mixing creates many UTXOs (CJ-16, CJ-17).
7. **Core Max drain to Shielded (#1205) and Max to an external shielded address (#1251) are missing.** Both are newer than the research.
8. **The identity endpoints are unowned:** top-up from Platform or Shielded, and credits → own Platform address (P IOS-069/070 say "Core only" / "not done").
9. **Shielded receive, shielded → external shielded, and external recipients for shield, unshield and withdraw are implied only.** X2's words say "shield/unshield", and today the desktop refuses shielded addresses (pay/model.ts:126).
10. **Advanced mode is undefined and contradicted:** X1b names it, but MG-00 D2 rejected a general advanced mode. Auto-enable and the gated set are unspecified, and on iOS Shielded and Send-tab Platform routes are not gated.
11. **Sync diagnostics are missing:** shielded sync info and the Platform-address list (IOS-114); only a loop-status row is planned (DP6-03 D10).
12. **Platform → Core withdrawal details are unplanned:** full vs partial form, fee headroom, asynchronous arrival; also the fee and timing sheets and tips (PADDR-16, PADDR-23).

## Stale research

- **03-ios-features.md:177** (move mixed coins: ≤500-input sweep only) is stale. Develop adds consolidation above 50 UTXOs and the 100-input asset-lock cap (#1221, merged 2026-10-27), plus finality and sync gates.
- **03-ios-features.md:175** (one-time recovery scan) is stale. Steady-state completion and the durable rescan intent after a store reset landed 2026-10-08/09 (`52a94122b`, `6ca4b8d7e`, `79417a150`).
- **03-ios-features.md:186 and dashpay/ios.md:188** (shielded "fee-aware Max") miss the Core → Shielded Max drain (#1205, 2026-10-21) and Max to an external shielded address (#1251, 2026-10-28).
- **03-ios-features.md:198 and dashpay/ios.md:205** (contested name 0.25 DASH) are stale. It is 0.15 DASH from protocol 14 (#1199, 2026-10-21). Desktop still locks 0.25 (registration_flow.rs:80).
- **dashpay/ios.md:205** (registration funding) predates the explicit source confirmation (#1141, merged 2026-10-08).
- **03-ios-features.md:143** ("Platform routes ADV") contradicts develop: Send-tab Platform routes are not gated (`SendViewModel.swift:662`). The gate (#1101) merged on 09-03, before the research, so this is an error rather than staleness.
- **Newer than both docs, not mentioned:**
  - restored-wallet block on Core-funded routes re-armed (#1238, 2026-10-23);
  - persistent failed-transfer toast (#1177, 2026-10-10);
  - newer-protocol banner (#1232, 2026-10-27) and the testnet PV14 pin, then auto-select (#1222, 2026-10-21).
- **Branch-only iOS work after the research** (not on develop): `perf/shielded-prover-warmup` (10-04..10-29), `fix/shielded-transfer-in-flight-row` (10-15..20), and `fix/shielded-username-denomination`, which sets the standard name's exit to 0.03 DASH (10-30). Develop still uses 0.1/0.25, which matches dashpay/ios.md:202.
- **Desktop parity.md is stale vs its own code:**
  - IOS-057 is "not started" (P:283), yet `coinjoin_recovery_scan`, `mixed_coins_sweep_plan` and `move_mixed_coins` "work" (m3-engine.md:108-111) and dw-app binds them;
  - QT-041…050 are "not started M3" (P:108-117), yet U-06 merged (3198a0a7);
  - IOS-028/030/035/054 point at superseded "M3/M4/M5" milestones with no ROADMAP owner.

# Part D: identity, usernames, marketplace, credits, voting, masternodes, branch-only

# Area D: identity, usernames, marketplace, credits, voting, masternodes, branch-only (iOS develop 41ab11aa2 vs desktop main 9e3add03)

Conventions. Desktop docs are in `~/workspace/dashwallet-desktop`; `DP1-09:135` means `docs/design/DP1-09-registration-wizard.md` line 135, `ROADMAP:216` means `docs/design/ROADMAP.md`, `DASHPAY:1131` means `docs/design/DASHPAY.md`, `MG-00:122` is `docs/design/MG-00-masternodes-ux.md`, `m4` is `docs/contracts/m4-dashpay-engine.md`, `mn-engine` is `docs/contracts/mn-engine.md`. "built:" means the code is on main (checked in rust/ or apps/). Merged rows share one line when their desktop mapping is identical.

Facts established while mapping, used throughout:

- **Desktop funding sources today.** The registration wizard funds from Core coins only (the send account's spendable coins, DP1-08 D9 at `DP1-08:116`; parity IOS-067 "Core funding only"). Invitation-funded registration was a deferred item (DP1-09 F3, `DP1-09:183`) and has since merged (DP5-02, DP5-03 merged at 7ed2a376). Platform-address funding is X1b (1.1, `ROADMAP:216`). Shielded funding is X2 (1.1, `ROADMAP:217`). X1a, X1b and X2 are not built (no commits, no `platform_address` or shielded code in `apps/desktop/src`). There are no design docs for X1a, X1b, X2, X3 or X4. No source-picker page or "funds are traceable" copy is specified anywhere.
- **Contested-name cost.** `rust/crates/dw-engine/src/platform/names.rs:648-665` prices the contest fund through rs-dpp `required_vote_resolution_fund_to_join` with the SDK's protocol version, so the fund follows the version. The asset lock is not version-aware: `registration_flow.rs:79-80` fixes `CONTESTED_LOCK_DUFFS = 25_000_000` (0.25 DASH, "as iOS locks", `m4:231`) and `registration.rs:436-445` uses `max(floor, credits/1000)`. The platform checkout confirms fund 0.2 DASH below PV14 (`fee/vote_resolution_fund_fees/v1.rs`) and 0.1 DASH from v2 (`v2.rs`). The standard lock is 0.03 DASH, same as iOS.
- **Identity-verify link.** Excluded as "no contract" (`DASHPAY:1108`, `DASHPAY:1131`). iOS `IdentityVerifyService.swift` (origin/develop, spot-checked) writes the document with the library's generic `wallet.createDocument` into the `identity-verify` contract. The contract ids are Android's registry values (mainnet `EVKMFboB3QBUa9Jo7PP5bsLyohzUz8zvw5c2gJs1SfcX`, testnet `Bhptm3yBDhLkRNt7ofjpwaBHhMUKjDrQoPufKzQaxmpK`). The contract is on-chain data, not part of a platform-repo pin (`git grep identity-verify` in ~/workspace/platform finds nothing, and that is expected). See NAME-08+16.
- **Parity rows are stale for MG and invitations.** `docs/parity.md` still marks QT-118..125/128..134 and IOS-080..082 as "parked" and IOS-077 as "routes only". On main, MG-01..MG-07, MG-09, MG-11, MG-04b slices and DP5-02/DP5-03 are merged. MG-10 (the My masternodes UI) and MG-13 are not built.

## 2. Identities screen

| ID | Story (as a user) | iOS evidence | iOS status | Desktop mapping | Gap |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|

## 4. Username marketplace

All rows map to X4 (1.2, `ROADMAP:219`; F20 `DASHPAY:1123`; "iOS-only, and the largest iOS DashPay screen; its name states last one session and need a mirror", `DASHPAY:614`). There is no X4 design doc, only the one-line ROADMAP row. Parity IOS-084 is "not started". The library surface exists (`docs/research/dashpay/platform.md:110`). The marketplace is not built.

## 6. Contested-username voting

Desktop mapping for the whole block: MG-07 (engine, built) and MG-11 (UI, built) under 1.1, optional (F21, `DASHPAY:1124`; `ROADMAP:233,238`). MG-11 is a tab "Username voting" on the Masternodes page, shown when Settings ▸ General ▸ Show Masternodes is on (default off, MG-09 D1 `MG-09-governance-ui.md:47`; MG-00 D1-D3). Parity IOS-079 is partial.

Notes on the counts.

- COVERED includes planned-but-unbuilt rows (X1b, X2, X4, MG-10, MG-13); I flagged each "planned, not built" in its Gap cell.
- EXCLUDED rows: NAME-08+16 (identity-verify link), VOTE-12+13 (bulk voting across contests), MN-11..15 (unban).
- N-A rows are stories where iOS has no feature (absent), or the desktop plan differs on purpose (More menu, advanced-mode gating, CoinJoin funding).
- DEAD rows: ID-30, CRED-12, BR-23 (NAME-22's testnet pin is noted inside a COVERED row).
- MISSING rows: NAME-06+07, NAME-23, VOTE-06, MN-09+10, CRED-04, CRED-08, BR-01..05, BR-08, BR-25.

## Top gaps in this area

1. **Funding-source picker and privacy page (ID-05, ID-08, ID-09, ID-12, NAME-05).** Desktop funds only from Core (parity IOS-067, `DP1-08:116`). Platform and Shielded sources are X1b/X2, both 1.1, with no source-picker UX, no "traceable to your username" warning and no readiness-blocker copy specified; X1a/X1b/X2 are not built and have no design docs.
2. **Shielded and Platform-address top-up, and credits↔address transfers (CRED-03, CRED-04, CRED-08).** X1b and X2 name only registration funding and internal transfer; top-up from address or pool, the two-step shielded top-up and "transfer credits to my Platform address" appear in no task row (parity IOS-069/070/062).
3. **Identity-verify link excluded on a stale reason (NAME-08+16, VOTE-06).** DASHPAY:1108/1131 say "no contract"; iOS ships it on mainnet and testnet via generic `createDocument` since #1141 (2026-10-08). Voters on both mobile wallets read it, and the desktop voting UI shows contenders only as shortened ids.
4. **Contested-name cost and invitation tier are not protocol-version aware at the lock (ID-07, ID-24, NAME-20).** Lock fixed at 0.25 DASH (`registration_flow.rs:80`) and the invitation contested threshold at 0.25 DASH (`invitation_check.rs:90`); iOS follows 0.15 DASH from PV14 (#1199, 2026-10-21). The contest fund itself is version-aware.
5. **Unban excluded (MN-11..15).** A product decision (CLAUDE.md:35, `DASHPAY:1145`, MG-00 D24) with real owner impact; iOS ships a guided unban, and the desktop derives the self-run operator key but never signs with it. Also MG-13 depends on an upstream platform-wallet change that is not in the pin.
6. **No newer-protocol advisory (NAME-23).** Nothing like iOS's #1232 banner; an unknown protocol version makes `cost_table` return `internal`.
7. **Marketplace scope unwritten (MKT-01..13, NAME-06+07, NAME-19).** X4 is one line, 1.2, no design doc. "Buy from the form", "register an additional name", the Browse tab and entries from Identities and My Profile have no owner.
8. **Evonode block activity and Nodes shortcut (MN-09+10).** MG-00:166 maps iOS's "epoch blocks" to governance epochs; evonode proposal activity has no task.
9. **No instant-username offer at confirmation, and no second-name or second-contest UI (NAME-10, NAME-18, NAME-19).** The offer lives in My Profile (DP1-10 D13); `register_name` supports extra names but no screen requests one.
10. **Voting-key import UI and bulk voting (VOTE-12+13, VOTE-17..19).** Engine attach is built; "Add key" is MG-10 (not built), and voting on several contests at once is explicitly not offered (MG-11 D23).
11. **Invitation entries (ID-22).** No invitation QR scan and no Home card for a stored pending invitation; only paste, OS link and the saved-links sheet.
12. **Contact payments from Platform/Shielded (BR-01..05, BR-08).** Branch-only on iOS, but X1b/X2 contain no contact-payment task.

## Stale research

- `docs/research/dashpay/android.md:313` leaves open whether iOS reads `identityVerify` documents; `DASHPAY:1108/1131` resolved it as "no contract". iOS #1141 (merged 2026-10-08; commits 08-24..10-07) publishes and reads the document on mainnet and testnet. `docs/research/dashpay/ios.md` lists `VerifyIdentityScreen` among "Legacy dead DashPay UI"; it is live on develop (NAME-08, NAME-16, VOTE-06).
- `docs/research/dashpay/ios.md` IOS-134 gives the auto-approve rule as "digits 2–9, hyphen or > 20 chars"; develop copy is "a number 2–9 or 20 or more characters" (ID-06).
- IOS-065/066/067/134 (join sheet, funding picker, form, instant-name offer, contested confirmation, Home/More status rows, "Rejected"/"Blocked" rows, voting-key import, vote change and 5-cast ceiling) were rebuilt or added in #1141 (2026-10-08) and #1162 (2026-10-08), after the 10-05/10-06 research reads.
- Contested-name cost: research and DP1-09 D4 assume "0.25 Dash". iOS #1199 (2026-10-21) follows the protocol version (0.15 DASH from PV14); the platform checkout confirms the fund 0.2 DASH → 0.1 DASH between fee versions (NAME-20, ID-07).
- Invitations: iOS #1156 (Home card, tiers, 2026-10-20) and #1173 (OneLink, 2026-10-20) postdate the research. The desktop handles OneLink generically (`deeplink.rs:469-485`).
- `docs/parity.md` (the desktop's own file, not research): IOS-077 still reads "`dw-uri::deeplink` routes invitation links; nothing else", although DP5-02 and DP5-03 are merged; QT-118..134 and IOS-080..082 read "parked" although the MG engine, MG-09 and MG-11 are merged (only MG-10 and MG-13 remain).
- #1232 (newer-protocol advisory, 2026-10-27) and #1222/#1232 protocol-pin churn (10-21..10-27) are newer than the research and have no desktop counterpart (NAME-22, NAME-23).

Abbreviations: ROADMAP = `docs/design/ROADMAP.md`; DASHPAY = `docs/design/DASHPAY.md`; parity = `docs/parity.md`; B1 = `docs/design/DECISIONS-PENDING.md` B1 (integration credentials). Facts used throughout:

- ROADMAP rows: MP-04 tax categories + CSV 1.2 (:252); MP-05 BIP70/72 1.2 "needed by DashSpend" (:253); MP-09 Explore (095-100) 1.2 (:257); MP-10 DashSpend CTX + PiggyCards (101-103), needs MP-05, MP-09, B1, "ships disabled before" (:258); MP-11 Buy & Sell Topper/Uphold/Coinbase/SwapKit (087-094), B1, 4 x L (:259); MP-12 ZenLedger (040) B1 (:260); MP-13 "CrowdNode, withdraw-only (091)" "1.2, or drop" (:261). ROADMAP:361: "1.2: X4, MP-04...09 and 13, MP-10...12 as B1 arrives | to be planned after 1.1".
- There are NO design docs for MP-04..MP-13 (`ls docs/design`: only BL/DP/E0/MG/U/X-less files). Every MP row is one line; sub-behaviours below are "named by the parity row" or "unspecified".
- Parity milestone labels (M5) are the old labels; ROADMAP's release column governs.
- The Tauri shell has Explore hidden: `EXPLORE_ENABLED = false` (`apps/desktop/src/components/shell.tsx:58`); U-01 D11: "Explore is hidden until its task" (`docs/design/U-01-shell.md:50`); route stub text "Explore is not available yet." (`apps/desktop/src/screens/Routes.tsx:31`).
- Tauri registers four OS URL schemes only: `dash`, `pay`, `dashwallet`, `dashpay` (`rust/crates/dw-app/src/shell/links.rs:47`). `dash-key:` / `dash-st:` are classified first (`rust/crates/dw-uri/src/deeplink.rs:125-130`, test `dashconnect_first` :512) and then refused: `LinkRoute::Unsupported{DashConnect}` (`links.rs:429`), UI text "DashConnect links are not supported" (`apps/desktop/src/app/shellBridge.ts:34`). A link containing "uphold" is refused as `Integration` (`deeplink.rs:131`, `links.rs:432`).

## DashConnect / App Connect (develop = v1 "two-QR"; branches = v2 "one-QR")

What the branch stack means for desktop (details also in Top gaps): every branch (`feat/dashconnect-v2` ... `feat/dashconnect-redesign-approve-sheet`, 12 branches, one stacked series) still gates on `!WalletEnvironment.isTestNetwork`, so nothing is mainnet on iOS either. The v2 protocol is a different protocol, not a patch: request v2 (`0x02`, TLVs, up to 4 scoped auth keys + 2 encryption keys, budget and expiry per key, 10 min staleness), the wallet registers a per-request session key itself (needs Platform protocol >= 14, no second `dash-st:` QR), on-chain disconnect, and (on `feat/app-connect-dashpay-carrier`) the `dashpay://connect?v=2&n=&r=&cb=` carrier because the App Connect DIP (dips#181) never allows `dash-key:` on mainnet. The desktop's own F24 row says "revisit with Connect v2" (DASHPAY.md:1127) but records nothing about what v2 is. Side facts from `~/workspace/connect-spec-2026-10-27/WALLET-SPEC.md` (not a desktop doc): Release 2 adds `dashpay://sign` (wallet signs and broadcasts any state transition an app hands it) and needs `dash-key:`/`dash-st:` removed before mainnet. Desktop-relevant consequences: (1) the desktop already registers the `dashpay` scheme, so a future `dashpay://connect` or `dashpay://sign` link will reach the app and today `classify_link` has no arm for it (`deeplink.rs:125-153`); (2) a desktop wallet is the natural signer for web dApps running in the same machine's browser (carrier `dashpay://connect` + `cb` return URL), which is the one place the desktop could be better than a phone; (3) v1 (`dash-key:`/`dash-st:`) is already a dead end: v2 mainnet will not use it, so building v1 for desktop would be throwaway.

Tools has 8 tabs, none is Connections (U-01 D10, U-01-shell.md:49). | EXCLUDED. No ROADMAP task, no design doc. Desktop's own Tools stays without the row. |

All desktop work is MP-09 (1.2). Explore is hidden in the Tauri sidebar until then (`shell.tsx:58`). There is no design doc, so filters, location, intro dialog and the "Explore menu" rows other than merchants/ATMs are only as specific as parity rows IOS-095..100.

## DashSpend (CTX, PiggyCards, gift cards)

All MP-10 (L + M, 1.2-window, "ships disabled before" B1; needs MP-05 BIP70/72 and MP-09). B1 names a CTX `X-Client-Id` (priority 2) but no PiggyCards credential, although MP-10 blocks both on B1 (DECISIONS-PENDING B1). iOS CTX uses header `X-Client-Id: dcg_ios`, staging host off mainnet; PiggyCards always uses production.

## Buy & Sell

All MP-11 ("per partner as B1 arrives", each 1 x L, "ships disabled"), B1 priorities: (3) Coinbase/Uphold redirect URIs "preferably an RFC 8252 loopback with PKCE"; (4) Topper and ZenLedger "with server-side signing or an accepted extraction risk" (iOS signs a Topper ES256 JWT in-app from a gitignored plist); (5) a SwapKit key. Desktop needs OS-scheme or loopback OAuth returns: today only a link containing "uphold" is classified and refused (`deeplink.rs:131`).

## CrowdNode

Plan: MP-13 "CrowdNode, withdraw-only (091)", release "1.2, or drop" (ROADMAP:261), no blocker; parity IOS-091 not started and still lists the full iOS scope: "tx-based signup, deposit, signed-message withdraw with limits, online-account link, APY, balance reminder..." (parity.md:317), which disagrees with the ROADMAP "withdraw-only". Older design: "CrowdNode and HWI behind flags" (`docs/design/DESIGN.md:26`). Parity IOS-030 CrowdNode grouped rows partial (parity.md:256); IOS-116 CrowdNode event notifications M5 (parity.md:342).

Branch `origin/feat/remove-crowdnode` (CN-16) deletes CrowdNode from iOS entirely (-29.8k lines, 266 files, 2026-10-30): the iOS team is leaning to "drop", the same fork as MP-13's "or drop". Desktop should record the decision (an iOS-restored wallet with a live CrowdNode balance has no in-app way out on an iOS build with the branch merged). Branch-only, not on develop.

## Area summary

Rows counted per Local ID in the inventory (merged rows distributed to their IDs): DashConnect 38 IDs, Explore 20, DashSpend 20, Buy & Sell 15, Swap 13, CrowdNode 16, Other 3 = 125 IDs.


Counts by Local ID (125 IDs; merged table rows expanded; 83 table rows): COVERED 45, PARTIAL 26, MISSING 3, EXCLUDED 40, N-A 4, DEAD 7.

Reading: the whole area is 1.2 (MP-09..13) or post-1.0 (F24); nothing is in 1.0 or 1.1. Almost every "COVERED" is covered by a one-line ROADMAP row with no design doc, so sub-behaviours are unspecified.

## Top gaps in this area

1. DashConnect v2 / App Connect is not recorded: F24 says only "revisit with Connect v2" (DASHPAY.md:1127); v2 is a different protocol (one-QR, per-key budgets, on-chain revoke, `dashpay://connect` carrier, `dashpay://sign` in Release 2) on 12 stacked iOS branches, testnet-only. Add a post-1.0 row recording these facts and that v1 `dash-key:`/`dash-st:` should not be built.
2. `dashpay://connect` / `dashpay://sign` will reach the desktop (it registers `dashpay`, `links.rs:47`) but `classify_link` has no arm (`deeplink.rs:125-153`); decide the refusal message now.
3. The desktop could be a DashConnect signer for web dApps on the same machine (carrier + `cb` return URL); this is the only place the desktop can beat a phone and it is entirely unplanned (needs identity-update signing and a budgeted session-key grant on the E0-04 model).
4. PV14 contest cost (iOS PR #1199, 2026-10-21): desktop hard-codes 0.25 DASH (`registration_flow.rs:79-80`, DP1-09 D4) but a contested registration costs 0.15 after PV14; the quote over-states and the Request-in-marketplace cost is also affected.
5. CrowdNode decision: MP-13 "withdraw-only ... or drop" vs parity IOS-091's full scope vs iOS branch `feat/remove-crowdnode` (deletion, 2026-10-30). No recorded decision; a restored iOS wallet with a CrowdNode balance has no desktop exit path unless MP-13 ships.
6. Testnet faucet: no tDASH shortcut in the Tauri app (only frozen Swift apps have the web shortcut, `ShortcutBarViewModel.swift:64-66`); in-app PoW faucet not planned (parity IOS-121 partial); Explore "Get Test Dash" not planned (IOS-095).
7. Swap history rows (iOS PR #1196, 2026-10-20) are a new MP-11 requirement absent from IOS-094; Sell/Buy rows, "Stuck/Not confirmed" labels and placeholder details.
8. Explore: "map links" (ROADMAP:257) vs iOS in-app map; "Nearby" has no desktop location source; the geo rules for PiggyCards (RU/CU) and Coinbase (GB) have no decided desktop detector.
9. Integration entry points: no Explore/Spend/ATM/Buy & Sell shortcuts or Payments "swap to crypto" card in the Tauri Home/Pay shell (U-03 D16, U-01 D10); MP-09/MP-11 do not say where they hang.
10. Internal Transfer tax category (iOS branch only) is not in MP-04 though X1b/X2 create own-fund transfers in 1.1, before tax categories in 1.2.
11. B1 omits a PiggyCards item while MP-10 is blocked on B1 as a whole; PiggyCards needs no key on iOS (production API), so MP-10 could be split.
12. SPEND-09 (record the order when CTX's ack is unclear) and SWAP safeguards (memo must ride the tx, minimum deposit, 80-byte OP_RETURN) are fund-loss invariants stated nowhere on the desktop side.

## Stale research

- `docs/research/dashpay/platform.md:229`: "DashPay Connect / `dashid:` browser login ... is not a shipped iOS feature; leave it out". Wrong: DashConnect v1 is shipped on iOS develop (Tools > Connections on every network, functional on testnet/devnet) since 2026-07-30 (PR #909 merged 2026-08-27; token purchase PR #1109; deep links PR #1104). It conflates DashConnect with the unhandled `dashid:` scheme. `03-ios-features.md` §1.13 (:234-245) is right about v1.
- `docs/research/dashpay/ios.md:252` (IOS-085 row): "DEC: Connect v2 supersedes; dips#191". The App Connect DIP is dashpay/dips#181 (inventory DC-B11; `~/workspace/connect-spec-2026-10-27/WALLET-SPEC.md:3`); also v2 is now concrete on iOS branches since 2026-10-08 (`feat/dashconnect-v2` ... `feat/dashconnect-redesign-approve-sheet`, tips 2026-10-31/11-01), none on develop. `ios.md:397` (open question 7 "Skip v1 on desktop?") has no answer recorded anywhere; F24 is the de facto answer.
- `docs/research/03-ios-features.md:390` and `ios.md:100` (CrowdNode "suspended/hidden"): the code hides only the entry points for users without an account; existing accounts keep portal, deposit, withdraw, reminders, notifications, leftover warnings. Newer still: `origin/feat/remove-crowdnode` (2026-10-30) deletes all of it.
- Not covered by either research doc (iOS merged after 2026-10-06): PR #1196 (2026-10-20) swap-order rows in history; PR #1199 (2026-10-21) contested-username cost follows protocol version (affects desktop registration quote, see EXP-16); PR #1218 nav-bar refactor (no behaviour). All DashConnect develop stories landed 2026-07-30..09-20 (before the research), so the research is accurate for develop; it is stale only against the branches.
- `docs/parity.md` IOS-085 ("dash-key login, dash-st state transitions ... (test networks)") describes only v1; IOS-086 and IOS-121 (faucet) are as stated, but the "partial" of IOS-121 refers to the frozen Swift apps, not the Tauri shell.

## Area summary

Counts over the 89 table rows (91 input rows; CON-41+CON-42 and CON-43+CON-44 are merged):

COVERED 54, PARTIAL 16, MISSING 3, EXCLUDED 2, N-A 2, DEAD 12 (total 89).

Reading: the contacts, profile and claim-side invitation stories are heavily planned and mostly built on the desktop (DP2/DP3/DP4/DP5 designs with GL evidence). The material gaps are all at the edges: paying a contact from Platform or Shielded funds (CON-40, branch-only on iOS and absent from the desktop plan), OS notification delivery and its toggle (NOTIF-07/10), and a few cosmetic or small items (public message, notification search, invitation QR scan, Home card for a pending invitation).

## Top gaps in this area

1. CON-40 pay a contact from Platform/Shielded balance: NOT IN PLAN (grepped any.?balance, DashPayWithdrawal; DASHPAY.md:341-345 keeps `Spend` Core/CoinJoin; X1b/X2 at ROADMAP.md:216-217 do not name contact payments). iOS branch-only (PR #1117, needs platform#4623, already in the desktop pin DASHPAY.md:379).
2. NOTIF-07 OS notifications for contact requests/accepts are not delivered on any OS: engine emits `DashPayNews` (bridge.rs:198), renderer only invalidates (events.ts:73), DP2-09 F4 deferred (DP2-09-contact-pane.md:96), W-02 covers Windows toasts only (ROADMAP.md:270).
3. NOTIF-10 no working notifications toggle or OS permission/denied flow in the Tauri app (General.tsx:142 "Not yet" row; U-04-settings.md:40).
4. CON-38 fiat entry for contact payments depends on MP-01 (ROADMAP.md:249), which is cut candidate 7 (ROADMAP.md:386) and not in DP3-06; no fiat code in `apps/desktop/src/screens/pay`.
5. CON-25 / PROF-15 the contact pane omits the contact's public message (DP2-09 F3 deferred, never picked up in DP4-03).
6. INV-04 no invitation-QR scan path (Add Contact scan rejects non-contact codes, DP2-08-add-contact.md:63,65); only link paste and OS handler.
7. INV-06 no persistent Home card for a pending invitation; "Links you opened" shows once per launch/unlock (DP5-01-link-intake.md:36).
8. INV-11 contested-name tier uses a fixed 0.25 DASH constant (registration_flow.rs:80) while iOS develop follows the protocol-version cost (#1199).
9. PROF-05/09/10 avatar file/gallery, crop and Imgur upload are built but disabled until B1 (DP4-03-edit-profile.md:63,103; DASHPAY.md:615): in practice only URL and Gravatar pictures can be set.
10. PROF-07 desktop's HTTPS-only avatar rule means contacts' iOS-published http:// or non-443 avatars never render (initials only).
11. NOTIF-03 notifications page has no search; CON-07 no pull-to-refresh; CON-12 no "send your first request" nudge (all cosmetic).
12. PROF-03 iOS lets About me run to 250 chars; contract/desktop limit is 140 (iOS-side latent mismatch, noted so desktop users are not surprised by long profiles on read).

- docs/research/03-ios-features.md:14 and docs/research/dashpay/ios.md:209-210,382: "Create / share / reclaim invitations" a pending decision; develop confirms removed (2026-07-29 scope) and the paste/scan form was deleted by #1156 (2026-10-20), leaving link/QR claim plus a Keychain-backed Home card. Research row IOS-077 ("paste / scan / link", ios.md:722) is stale for paste.
- docs/research/dashpay/ios.md:358 names `dashpaytest.onelink.me`; develop #1173 (2026-10-20) added the mainnet `dashpay.onelink.me` entitlement and a OneLink unwrapping normalizer (`af_dp`, `deep_link_value`, `link`, `af_sub1`).
- ios.md:176 IOS-050 and DASHPAY F14 describe `ConfirmContactSendSheet` from Core: develop still ships Core-only, placeholder-fee; the exact-fee review (PR #1214, 2026-10-15..30), fiat amount (fix/contact-pay-fiat, MO-1100) and any-balance pay (PR #1117, 2026-10-06..29) are unmerged branches newer than the research (2026-10-06).
- ios.md:220 / DASHPAY F16: notifications feed "New / Earlier / Pending, search"; develop has no Pending header (e7a97cc72, 2026-08-28) and local DashPay pushes are narrow (<=10 min, never frontmost, background refresh off since 370f6724d 2026-09-24; tap is a no-op).
- docs/parity.md:276,299,300,303: IOS-050/073/074 "no avatars (DP4)" and IOS-077 "dw-uri only" no longer match main (DP4-03 and DP5-01..03 landed); parity IOS-075 "Pending" header mirrors the stale iOS research.
- iOS #1199 (contested-name tier follows protocol version) post-dates the desktop's fixed 25_000_000-duff constant (registration_flow.rs:80).
